# Injection audit for the admin apps and workers

This table records the input boundaries inspected in this change and the checks used to verify them. AI tests use fixtures and mocked bindings. Network tests use local services or mocked fetches.

| Component | Input | Risk | Admin API status notifications | Admin-entered webhook URL and stored destination read by the queue consumer | Unsafe scheme, local hostname, nonstandard port and redirects | Fixed. The URL is validated at registration and again at send time and the delivery fetch handles redirects manually. | `apps/admin-api/test/status-notify.test.ts` |
| Status | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Theme packages | ZIP file headers, names, offsets and file data | Path traversal, aliases, non-regular entries, overlapping metadata, excess decompression | Fixed. The reader bounds the archive, entry count and total decompression. It checks relative paths, duplicates, file modes, header agreement and CRC. | `packages/theme-packages/test/package.test.ts` |
| Admin API themes | `POST /api/themes/import`, `id`, `newId`, `theme.json` and assets | Untrusted metadata or active content stored in a draft, unbounded request bodies | Fixed. The admin web proxy and API both limit the streamed upload. The Worker proxy refuses upstream redirects. The API checks the package schema and checks each file and the aggregate asset size before creating the draft. | `apps/admin-api/test/theme-packages.test.ts`, `apps/admin-web/test/theme-proxy.test.ts` |
| Admin API themes | Draft asset read, write, delete and published asset delivery | R2 path selection, SVG execution, response content type | Fixed. Reads and writes restrict asset names and SVG delivery adds a sandbox policy. Individual uploads and ZIP imports enforce the same asset checks. | `apps/admin-api/test/theme-packages.test.ts` |
| Admin API themes | `GET /api/themes/:id/export` filename and version | Header injection | OK. The export reads an existing theme ID and accepts the draft or a numeric version string. The filename is assembled from those fields. | `apps/admin-api/test/theme-packages.test.ts` |
| Theme packages | CSS-valued schema fields, token references, asset paths, SVG markup | CSS rule or resource injection, executable SVG, external requests | Fixed. CSS values reject comments, raw URLs and quoted image sources. Asset references use the package file grammar. SVGs accept passive elements and local paint references. | `packages/theme-packages/test/package.test.ts` and `themes:check` |
| Admin web theme builder | Live editor state and SVG art read by the preview | Rendering CSS from an invalid draft | Fixed. The preview gates CSS and asset fetching on validation, checks SVG before compiling it, and disables scripting in its iframe. | Theme validator regression tests and admin web type-check |
| Admin web site theme | Published theme metadata from the admin API | SSRF via CSS resource URLs | Fixed. The web app builds the CSS request from the configured admin API origin and validated package ID and version. Neither theme request follows redirects. | `apps/admin-web/test/shell.test.ts` |
| Admin API search | Error search, moderation queue, staff names, contact inbox and invites | SQL and LIKE pattern injection | OK. The reviewed predicates bind parameters and escape wildcard characters in literal searches. | `apps/admin-api/test/errors.test.ts`, `contact-inbox.test.ts` and `audit-feed.test.ts` |
| Admin API contact intake | Sender email, message and captured page URL | Header injection or untrusted navigation in the inbox | OK. The contact parser bounds text and validates email. The page URL must have a supported scheme and platform host. React renders the message as text. | `apps/admin-api/test/contact-submit.test.ts` and `contact-inbox.test.ts` |
| Admin web authentication | Callback state, return path and refresh continuation | Open redirect | OK. The login flow checks a same-origin return path before using it. | Admin web login and passkey tests |
| Cost monitor | Queue work version, job kind, timestamp and calendar day | Coercion of an unrecognised job and malformed time windows | Fixed. Job names require strings from the declared set and date inputs use the supported format and range. | `apps/cost-monitor/test/costs.test.ts` |
| Cost monitor | Scheduled collection and provider usage replies | GraphQL and SQL injection | OK for the reviewed collection queries. The GraphQL document is constant with separate variables. SQL data values are bound and the product cost column has two fixed choices. | `apps/cost-monitor/test/costs.test.ts` |
| Log service | `POST /log` and `POST /log/batch` | Filesystem path selection, forged log records | Fixed. Single and batch writes use the same app name and level validation and encode control characters on one physical line. | `apps/log-service/src/index.test.ts` |
| Log service | `GET /logs/:appName`, snapshots, SSE and viewer | File path selection, HTML injection | Fixed. Direct reads check the filename grammar. Aggregate reads select among files already enumerated in the log directory and SSE writes JSON. The viewer inserts entry fields as text. | `apps/log-service/src/index.test.ts` and viewer source review |
| Logger | Node file logging and structured console entries | Log splitting, local filename injection | Fixed. File output validates the app name and encodes controls. Console output remains a structured object. | Logger build and log-service record regression |
| MCP log server | Tool filters and log service replies | Unvalidated argument types or query injection | Fixed. Per-tool schemas check keys, types and bounds before accessing the configured log service. Queries use URLSearchParams. | packages/mcp-log-server/test/input.test.ts |
| Media worker | Queued live recording URL and container VOD requests | SSRF, redirects and reads outside the recording | Fixed. Initial playback uses an HTTPS delivery host. Outbound VOD fetches disallow redirects and require regular relative path segments. | `apps/media/test/live-review.test.ts` |
| Media worker | Internal job path/commands, private rendition and download paths | R2 selection and unauthorised mutations | OK for the reviewed key boundaries. Internal job endpoints require a shared key and a valid video ID. Private media uses signed path claims and constrained keys. | `apps/media/test/internal-jobs.test.ts` and media suite |
| Processor review | Video thumbnail and image-review queue URLs | SSRF and redirect following from image downloads | Fixed. Both review paths use a single fetcher with an HTTPS delivery allowlist and disallow redirects. | `apps/processor/src/__tests__/review-consumer.test.ts` |
| Processor moderation | Text passed to the review classifier and classifier responses | Content treated as instructions, incomplete verdict accepted | Fixed. Policy text is wrapped in a data field with escaped delimiters. Review scores cover the configured categories and unrecognised Llama Guard output takes the error path. | `apps/processor/src/__tests__/review-consumer.test.ts` and `moderation-units.test.ts` |
| Processor push | Stored WNS channels and Web Push subscriptions | Server request forgery, credential forwarding via redirects | Fixed. Outbound senders verify provider HTTPS hosts and disallow redirects. | `apps/processor/src/__tests__/push-senders.test.ts` and `push-webpush.test.ts` |
| Scheduler | Scheduled R2 documents and `POST /failed/retry` | Document selects a different video or schedule key | Fixed. The video ID and timestamp must agree with the key read before the worker publishes or retries. | `apps/scheduler/test/scheduler.test.ts` |
| Short links | Target path and forwarded query parameters | Scheme or destination injection | OK. The path identifies a typed entity or the home page. The worker chooses a configured origin, encodes IDs and forwards only the share parameter allowlist. | `apps/short-links/test/routes.test.ts` |
| Status | `/en/*` canonical redirect | Path treated as an external destination | Fixed. The worker sets a pathname on its own origin. | `apps/status/test/subscriptions.test.ts` |
| Status | `/subscribe`, `/subscribe/confirm`, `/unsubscribe` form and token inputs | SQL injection and HTML injection | OK for the reviewed form fields. Email and token shape are checked, D1 operations bind values and the renderer escapes text. | `apps/status/test/subscriptions.test.ts` |
| Status | Cron playback probe data from the public videos API | SSRF or media path injection | Fixed. R2 IDs and revisions use the media key grammar. Bunny probes accept only the configured HTTPS delivery hosts and use manual redirects. | `apps/status/test/status.test.ts` |
| WebSocket server | `authenticate`, `subscribe`, `unsubscribe`, `message`, `ping`, `heartbeat` | Oversized or malformed frames, unstructured channel names, missing data, unauthorised subscription or publish | Fixed. Client messages use a bounded discriminated schema. Existing token expiry, public feed exceptions, private channel ownership and server-only channel rules remain covered. | `apps/websocket-server/test/websocket.test.ts` and `admin-live.test.ts` |
| WebSocket server | Worker and DO fan-out, ticket, view, live viewer and QoE routes | Untrusted fan-out and access to internal topics | OK for the reviewed auth boundary. Internal routes verify the key before dispatching, and topic-directory calls use a service binding. | `apps/websocket-server/test/internal-key.test.ts`, `tickets.test.ts` and `topic-router.test.ts` |
| Shared types | Media key grammar, Bunny library hosts and short-link target parsing | Unsafe handoff between workers | Fixed for CDN host selection. Shared key and target parsing remain the input contracts reused by the callers above. | Media, status and short-links tests |
| Processor | Generic queue envelope before archiving | R2 key and malformed archive fields | Fixed. Message IDs, types, dates and payload object shape checked before the archive write. | apps/processor/src/__tests__/messageProcessor.test.ts |
| WebSocket server | Worker and DO broadcast input | Invalid channel or batch reaches fan-out | Fixed. The Worker and DO share a channel and batch schema. | apps/websocket-server/test/internal-key.test.ts |
| Media container | JSON commands | Bad or oversized JSON enters job dispatch | Fixed. The container uses one bounded JSON object parser on every job route. | apps/media/test/container-request.test.ts |

## Code inventory

The admin API inventory combines the router registration syntax tree with the runtime Hono route registry. Routes from factories, loops and Chanfana are included. Middleware registrations are counted separately. The admin web inventory uses App Router files, server action exports and JSX submit sites. Queue and cron entries come from the Wrangler configurations, including stage and QA.

The inventory records 485 Hono registrations, 2 Worker-level robots entries, 29 admin web handler methods, 80 pages, 80 JSX submit sites, 337 server action exports, 39 queue names and 11 worker/cron pairs. Route and form rows name the injection boundary that applies to the entry. The findings table above describes the changes and regression tests at shared boundaries.

| Log service | `GET /openapi.yaml` | Structured output | OK | Serializes the registered schemas. [index.ts](../../apps/log-service/src/index.ts) |
| Log service | `GET /health` | JSON | OK | Returns service state without reading path or body fields. [index.ts](../../apps/log-service/src/index.ts) |
| Log service | `POST /log` | Path and log injection | Fixed | Filename grammar, level validation, and single physical line formatting. index.test.ts tests the route. [index.ts](../../apps/log-service/src/index.ts) |
| Log service | `POST /log/batch` | Path and log injection | Fixed | Bounded batch and the same per-entry rules as the single-write route. index.test.ts tests the route. [index.ts](../../apps/log-service/src/index.ts) |
| Log service | `GET /logs` | File access | OK | Lists the configured log directory without a caller-supplied path. [index.ts](../../apps/log-service/src/index.ts) |

| Log service | `GET /logs/stream` | Search and file access | Fixed | Filters select only files enumerated in the configured directory and return JSON. Filters accept a bounded line count and enumerated switches where applicable. [index.ts](../../apps/log-service/src/index.ts) |
| Log service | `GET /logs/stream/realtime` | SSE injection | Fixed | File names come from directory enumeration. Records are JSON-stringified before SSE framing. Filters accept a bounded line count and enumerated switches where applicable. [index.ts](../../apps/log-service/src/index.ts) |
| Log service | `GET /viewer` | DOM injection | Fixed | Static viewer inserts record fields as DOM text. [index.ts](../../apps/log-service/src/index.ts) |

| WebSocket DO | `unsubscribe` | Frame shape and authorization | Fixed | Validated channel and only the sending socket’s subscriptions. [websocket.test.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/test/websocket.test.ts) |
| WebSocket DO | `message` | Frame shape and authorization | Fixed | Bounded validated channel and JSON data, plus live token and channel publish checks. [websocket.test.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/test/websocket.test.ts) |
| WebSocket DO | `ping` | Frame shape and authorization | Fixed | Numeric timestamp only. The reply carries the server time. [websocket.test.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/test/websocket.test.ts) |
| WebSocket DO | `heartbeat` | Frame shape and authorization | Fixed | Numeric timestamp only. The reply carries the server time. [websocket.test.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/test/websocket.test.ts) |
| MCP log server | `get_logs` | Argument shape and query construction | Fixed | Validates object keys, line count, app and level. URLSearchParams builds the filter. [index.ts](../../packages/mcp-log-server/src/index.ts) |
| MCP log server | `watch_logs` | Argument shape and streaming | Fixed | Validates app, level and duration before opening the configured SSE endpoint. [index.ts](../../packages/mcp-log-server/src/index.ts) |
| MCP log server | `list_log_apps` | Argument shape | Fixed | Only an empty argument object is accepted. The log service lists its fixed directory. [index.ts](../../packages/mcp-log-server/src/index.ts) |
| MCP log server | `search_logs` | Query text | Fixed | Bounds query/limits and uses literal substring comparison. Tool results remain text. [index.ts](../../packages/mcp-log-server/src/index.ts) |
| Logger | `Logger.trace/debug/info/warn/error` | Log data and filename | Fixed | Redaction and structured console output. Local file logging validates app names and escapes control characters. [index.ts](../../packages/logger/src/index.ts) |
| Logger | `Logger.request` | Log summary fields | OK | Fixed severity policy, structured data and central redaction. [index.ts](../../packages/logger/src/index.ts) |
| Logger | `Logger.job` | Log summary fields | OK | Fixed severity policy, structured data and central redaction. [index.ts](../../packages/logger/src/index.ts) |
| Logger | `resolveLogServiceUrl/configureLogging` | Runtime config | OK | Only deployment/process settings decide the local log collector and level. [index.ts](../../packages/logger/src/index.ts) |
| Theme packages | `readZip` | Files, limits and archive metadata | Fixed | Size, mode, path, duplicate, format and CRC checks before files become assets. package.test.ts covers these controls. [zip.ts](../../packages/theme-packages/src/zip.ts) |
| Theme packages | `zipToPackage` | Archive layout, files and theme.json | Fixed | Only one theme.json and bounded archive entries. The import endpoint checks the package schema before storage. [zip.ts](../../packages/theme-packages/src/zip.ts) |
| Theme packages | `packageToZip/createZip` | Export fields | OK | Export uses the known theme ID and package asset names. ZIP headers derive from the emitted byte arrays. [zip.ts](../../packages/theme-packages/src/zip.ts) |
| Theme packages | `validatePackage` | Theme JSON, SVG and CSS | Fixed | Schema checks, package asset grammar and passive SVG/CSS safety rules before publish or preview. [validate.ts](../../packages/theme-packages/src/validate.ts) |
| Theme packages | `compilePackage` | Validated theme and platform asset URL resolver | Fixed | Callers validate before publishing/previewing. Compiler emits scoped CSS from the known fields. [validate.ts](../../packages/theme-packages/src/validate.ts) |
| Admin imports | `Video batch and item metadata` | D1 and JSON | OK | parseNewItem and parseNewChannel select known fields, then store metadata through bound statements and the fixed videos API. [adminImport.router.ts](../../apps/admin-api/src/routers/adminImport.router.ts) |
| Admin imports | `Sidecars and translations` | File metadata and caption text | OK | Known sidecar fields, capped languages/text and video ID lookup. Forwarded as JSON to the videos service. [adminImport.router.ts](../../apps/admin-api/src/routers/adminImport.router.ts) |
| Admin imports | `Prepared rendition file` | R2 keys and playlist import | OK | Validated staging ID, track/rung/file grammar and authorized video job. Transcoder checks the prepared playlists before using them. [adminImport.router.ts](../../apps/admin-api/src/routers/adminImport.router.ts) |
| Admin imports | `Takeout register/upload/confirm` | File import and R2 keys | OK | Source/file IDs checked and staged file/metadata processed via the videos service. [adminImport.router.ts](../../apps/admin-api/src/routers/adminImport.router.ts) |

| admin-web | `RootLayout active CSS` | Raw style HTML and SSRF | Fixed | Versioned CSS is fetched only from the configured admin API. acceptPackageCss checks size, scope and HTML delimiters before insertion. [layout.tsx](../../apps/admin-web/src/app/layout.tsx) |
| log-service | `Log viewer historical snapshot and SSE` | DOM XSS | Fixed | Dynamic app name, level and message use textContent. CSS class uses a known level. [viewer.html](../../apps/log-service/public/viewer.html) |

| admin-web | `Contact message link, email and body` | URL and markup | OK | User text renders as text. Intake bounds and validates email and restricts page URL to platform hosts. [page.tsx](../../apps/admin-web/src/app/(protected)/contact/[id]/page.tsx) |
| media | `Staged HLS media playlists` | Path and protocol selection | OK | Parses playlist segments using a scoped staging grammar and Worker outbound handlers. [prebuilt.mjs](../../apps/media/container/prebuilt.mjs) |
| processor | `Quran corpus snapshot imports` | Structured record parsing | OK | The configured Quran service produces delimited records and the consumer applies record schema and known output keys. [snapshot-stream.ts](../../apps/processor/src/quran/snapshot-stream.ts) |
| shared-types | `Short-link path and query parser` | Redirect construction | OK | Typed kinds, anchored IDs/handles and selected query fields. [short-links.ts](../../packages/shared-types/src/short-links.ts) |
| shared-types | `Media upload and playback key contracts` | Path and host restrictions | Fixed | Anchored media ID, revision and media-file grammars and the known delivery host table. [media-delivery.ts](../../packages/shared-types/src/media-delivery.ts) |
| shared-types | `Page/email text block markup and links` | HTML and link injection | OK | Renderer escapes text and asks the caller’s link policy before creating an anchor. [page-blocks.ts](../../packages/shared-types/src/page-blocks.ts) |
| shared-types | `Community post attachment and text inputs` | Structured input and link routing | OK | Discriminated post shapes and validated media fields delegate persistence to the application. [post-schema.ts](../../packages/shared-types/src/post-schema.ts) |

### Durable Object lifecycle and callbacks

| Component | Input | Risk | Status | Control and source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| websocket-server | `fetch upgrade` | WebSocket handshake | OK | The worker chooses the shard and the DO accepts only a WebSocket upgrade. [websocket.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/src/websocket.ts) |
| websocket-server | `webSocketClose` | Close metadata | OK | Detaches only the socket that closed. Core association comes from the server attachment. [websocket.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/src/websocket.ts) |
| websocket-server | `webSocketError` | Error metadata | OK | Uses structured error reporting and socket-local cleanup. [websocket.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/src/websocket.ts) |
| websocket-server | `alarm` | Clock-driven dispatch | OK | Flushes current view and coalescer state. Object key and channel were validated at inbound dispatch. [websocket.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/src/websocket.ts) |
| websocket-server | `ViewCounter.increment / alarm` | D1 identifiers | OK | The DO validates video/shard inputs before using fixed counter queries with bound values. [view-counter.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/src/view-counter.ts) |
| websocket-server | `PostViewCounter.increment / alarm` | D1 identifiers | OK | The DO validates post/shard inputs before updating counters with bound values. [post-view-counter.ts](../../apps/websocket-server/src/post-view-counter.ts) |
| media | `container control event` | Job event and result | OK | The outbound control request must match the assigned job ID. Event kind governs updates to the current DO job. [transcoder.ts](../../apps/media/src/transcoder.ts) |
